### November 14 research: native VL53L4CD address invariant in generated wiring

Owner: Robot Open Source Research. Reconciled PR #17 at 2006 commits before write, 65 issue comments, 65 merged discussion entries, zero submitted reviews, zero review threads. Inspected current TofBus mux selection/readback and host fake regressions, plus the existing carrier logic-level gate and wiring manifest. No duplicate bus driver or second I2C controller introduced.

Source improvement: commit 6033facf9a88766bc3fa1d9890513d090e1b2fa8 adds a fail-closed wiring-manifest check requiring exactly one VL53L4CD at native 7-bit address 0x29 on each of eight isolated range mux lanes. Existing per-lane uniqueness and position checks alone permitted a changed device address or extra different-address device to appear in the generated assembly signal harness without rejection. This is a manifest integrity guard, NOT hardware address discovery, sensor identity proof or physical validation. Tests NOT EXECUTED; QA owner should run the wiring manifest generator against the actual plan and add a negative mutation regression for wrong address and extra range device. Check current branch head before further edits.

Upstream comparison: ST UM2931 https://www.st.com/resource/en/user_manual/um2931-a-guide-to-using-the-vl53l4cd-ultra-lite-driver-uld-stmicroelectronics.pdf documents independent XSHUT sequencing and address reassignment for shared unisolated I2C. Existing project instead isolates each native-address sensor through TCA9548A and verifies mux channel readback, so adding XSHUT pin allocation or another library would be redundant and risky. Pololu Arduino driver https://github.com/pololu/vl53l4cd-arduino exposes setAddress and range status but explicitly notes weaker error checking than ST ULD; current code retains its pinned driver and fail-closed mux policy. LidarArray https://github.com/joaoaugustocz/LidarArray offers MIT-licensed PCF8574 XSHUT array management, but replacing mux isolation would change wiring and safety validation with no demonstrated gain. No upstream code copied; verify exact pinned source notices before distribution. Physical carrier VIN and SDA/SCL voltage remain unqualified as recorded in docs/research/2026-11-13-vl53l4cd-carrier-logic-level-gate.md. Status SOURCE_COMMITTED, NOT_TESTED; next QA action is executable manifest negative tests, then instrumented bus and sensor tests on actual carriers.

### November 15 research: planar AprilTag pose ambiguity and reuse decision

Owner: Robot Open Source Research. Reconciled PR #17 head 35c73140c1bbe48d5b37ba2b223768625c10ab34, 66 issue comments and 66 merged discussion entries, zero submitted reviews and zero review threads. Latest actionable physical dock registration comment: https://github.com/hungngodev/desk-robot-remix/pull/17#issuecomment-6888068146 . Compared current code/laptop/src/desk_robot/dock_vision.py and code/tests/test_dock_vision.py; existing code already checks corner convexity, front-facing tag normal, positive range, reprojection, calibration and chassis offset, so do not reimplement those guards.

Unresolved integration concern: planar four-corner IPPE pose estimation has two possible pose solutions, particularly hard to distinguish for small distant markers. OpenCV documents that SOLVEPNP_IPPE_SQUARE produces two solutions via solvePnPGeneric and requires the existing exact four model-point order: https://docs.opencv.org/4.5.1/d9/d0c/group__calib3d.html . Current adapter calls solvePnP for one result and does not compare second-solution reprojection or temporal agreement. AprilRobotics AprilTag 3 (https://github.com/AprilRobotics/apriltag) is a mature upstream alternative with a small C detector, pose estimation and documented Linux builds; its native build and Python bindings would add packaging, ABI and integration complexity to the existing Mac OpenCV stack. The Apache 2.0 ROS 2 wrapper https://github.com/open-feach/apriltag3_ros exposes orthogonal iteration and covariance but introduces a ROS runtime dependency unnecessary for this single Mac brain. These are evaluated references only; no source was copied or license inherited by this note. Confirm actual upstream license files before any vendor or redistribution decision.

Recommendation: retain existing OpenCV ArUco AprilTag 36h11 and IPPE_SQUARE owner. First add a narrow injected regression proving an ambiguous pair of valid front-facing PnP solutions cannot independently grant dock approach; then evaluate solvePnPGeneric and a conservative ambiguity gate with calibrated real JPEG sequences. Do not select a numeric ambiguity threshold without measured false hold and false approach rates at the actual 60 mm marker, XIAO optics, range and lighting. Cross-check physical WROOM dual terminal IR freshness, contact debounce, independent motor inhibit and charger isolation before any docking authorization. Status RESEARCH_RECORDED, NO_SOURCE_CHANGE, TESTS_NOT_EXECUTED, HARDWARE_NOT_QUALIFIED. Next owner: Robot Implementation Loop for bounded source/test integration; QA for exact-head dock vision/runtime tests; hardware owner for measured optical and electrical acceptance. Keep PR draft.
